default_acl: audit r app_runtime=r/app_migrator
default_acl: public S app_runtime=rU/app_migrator
default_acl: public r app_runtime=arwd/app_migrator
default_acl: reporting r app_runtime=r/app_migrator,app_reporting=r/app_migrator

function: CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION audit.prevent_modification() RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $function$ BEGIN IF current_setting('app.audit_maintenance', true) = 'on' THEN RETURN NULL; END IF; RAISE EXCEPTION 'Audit records are append-only: %.%', TG_TABLE_SCHEMA, TG_TABLE_NAME USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege'; END $function$
function: CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION audit.record_change() RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'pg_temp' AS $function$ DECLARE old_row jsonb; new_row jsonb; changed text[]; redacted_column text; BEGIN IF TG_LEVEL = 'STATEMENT' THEN INSERT INTO audit.record_changes (table_schema, table_name, operation, user_id, actor, request_id, database_user) VALUES (TG_TABLE_SCHEMA, TG_TABLE_NAME, TG_OP, util.current_user_id(), util.current_actor(), util.current_request_id(), session_user); RETURN NULL; END IF; IF TG_OP IN ('UPDATE', 'DELETE') THEN old_row := to_jsonb(OLD); END IF; IF TG_OP IN ('INSERT', 'UPDATE') THEN new_row := to_jsonb(NEW); END IF; IF TG_OP = 'UPDATE' THEN SELECT array_agg(n.key ORDER BY n.key) INTO changed FROM jsonb_each(new_row) AS n WHERE n.value IS DISTINCT FROM old_row -> n.key; IF changed IS NULL THEN RETURN NULL; END IF; END IF; IF TG_NARGS > 0 THEN FOREACH redacted_column IN ARRAY TG_ARGV LOOP IF old_row IS NOT NULL AND jsonb_typeof(old_row -> redacted_column) <> 'null' THEN old_row := jsonb_set(old_row, ARRAY[redacted_column], '"[redacted]"'); END IF; IF new_row IS NOT NULL AND jsonb_typeof(new_row -> redacted_column) <> 'null' THEN new_row := jsonb_set(new_row, ARRAY[redacted_column], '"[redacted]"'); END IF; END LOOP; END IF; INSERT INTO audit.record_changes ( table_schema, table_name, operation, record_id, old_values, new_values, changed_columns, user_id, actor, request_id, database_user ) VALUES ( TG_TABLE_SCHEMA, TG_TABLE_NAME, TG_OP, COALESCE(new_row ->> 'id', old_row ->> 'id'), old_row, new_row, to_jsonb(changed), util.current_user_id(), util.current_actor(), util.current_request_id(), session_user ); RETURN NULL; END $function$
function: CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION audit.row_hash_of(content jsonb) RETURNS character LANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE PARALLEL SAFE AS $function$ SELECT encode(sha256(convert_to((content - 'row_hash' - 'chain_hash' - 'sealed_at')::text, 'UTF8')), 'hex') $function$
function: CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION audit.seal(target regclass) RETURNS bigint LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $function$ DECLARE previous text; sealed bigint := 0; r record; BEGIN PERFORM set_config('app.audit_maintenance', 'on', true); EXECUTE format('SELECT chain_hash FROM %s WHERE chain_hash IS NOT NULL ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1', target) INTO previous; FOR r IN EXECUTE format('SELECT id, row_hash FROM %s WHERE chain_hash IS NULL ORDER BY id', target) LOOP previous := encode(sha256(convert_to(coalesce(previous, '') || r.row_hash, 'UTF8')), 'hex'); EXECUTE format('UPDATE %s SET chain_hash = $1, sealed_at = now() WHERE id = $2', target) USING previous, r.id; sealed := sealed + 1; END LOOP; RETURN sealed; END $function$
function: CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION audit.set_row_hash() RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $function$ BEGIN NEW.chain_hash := NULL; NEW.sealed_at := NULL; NEW.row_hash := audit.row_hash_of(to_jsonb(NEW)); RETURN NEW; END $function$
function: CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION audit.verify_chain(target regclass, OUT checked bigint, OUT sealed bigint, OUT row_mismatches bigint, OUT chain_mismatches bigint, OUT first_bad_id bigint) RETURNS record LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $function$ DECLARE previous text := NULL; first boolean := true; r record; expected text; BEGIN checked := 0; sealed := 0; row_mismatches := 0; chain_mismatches := 0; first_bad_id := NULL; FOR r IN EXECUTE format('SELECT id, row_hash, chain_hash, audit.row_hash_of(to_jsonb(t)) AS actual FROM %s AS t ORDER BY id', target) LOOP checked := checked + 1; IF r.actual IS DISTINCT FROM r.row_hash THEN row_mismatches := row_mismatches + 1; first_bad_id := coalesce(first_bad_id, r.id); END IF; IF r.chain_hash IS NOT NULL THEN sealed := sealed + 1; IF NOT first THEN expected := encode(sha256(convert_to(coalesce(previous, '') || r.row_hash, 'UTF8')), 'hex'); IF expected IS DISTINCT FROM r.chain_hash THEN chain_mismatches := chain_mismatches + 1; first_bad_id := coalesce(first_bad_id, r.id); END IF; END IF; previous := r.chain_hash; first := false; END IF; END LOOP; END $function$
function: CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION util.additional_program_request_record_ids() RETURNS bigint[] LANGUAGE sql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'pg_temp' AS $function$ SELECT COALESCE(array_agg(DISTINCT r.student_record_id), ARRAY[]::bigint[]) FROM public.additional_program_requests AS r WHERE r.status = 'requested' AND ( util.has_permission('additional_programs.approve') OR r.org_unit_id = ANY (util.permission_org_unit_ids('additional_programs.approve')) OR r.program_id = ANY (util.permission_scope_ids('additional_programs.approve', 'program')) ) $function$
function: CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION util.current_actor() RETURNS text LANGUAGE sql STABLE PARALLEL SAFE AS $function$ SELECT NULLIF(current_setting('app.actor', true), '') $function$
function: CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION util.current_person_id() RETURNS bigint LANGUAGE sql STABLE PARALLEL SAFE AS $function$ SELECT u.person_id FROM public.users AS u WHERE u.id = util.current_user_id() $function$
function: CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION util.current_request_id() RETURNS text LANGUAGE sql STABLE PARALLEL SAFE AS $function$ SELECT NULLIF(current_setting('app.request_id', true), '') $function$
function: CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION util.current_user_id() RETURNS bigint LANGUAGE sql STABLE PARALLEL SAFE AS $function$ SELECT NULLIF(current_setting('app.user_id', true), '')::bigint $function$
function: CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION util.enable_audit(target regclass, redacted_columns text[] DEFAULT '{}'::text[], ignored_columns text[] DEFAULT '{}'::text[]) RETURNS void LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $function$ DECLARE table_name text; arguments text; ignored text; BEGIN SELECT c.relname INTO table_name FROM pg_class AS c WHERE c.oid = target; SELECT COALESCE(string_agg(quote_literal(c), ', '), '') INTO arguments FROM unnest(redacted_columns) AS c; EXECUTE format('DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS %I ON %s', table_name || '_after_write_audit', target); EXECUTE format('DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS %I ON %s', table_name || '_after_update_audit', target); IF cardinality(ignored_columns) = 0 THEN EXECUTE format( 'CREATE TRIGGER %I AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE OR DELETE ON %s FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION audit.record_change(%s)', table_name || '_after_write_audit', target, arguments ); ELSE ignored := quote_literal(ignored_columns::text) || '::text[]'; EXECUTE format( 'CREATE TRIGGER %I AFTER INSERT OR DELETE ON %s FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION audit.record_change(%s)', table_name || '_after_write_audit', target, arguments ); EXECUTE format( 'CREATE TRIGGER %I AFTER UPDATE ON %s FOR EACH ROW WHEN ((to_jsonb(OLD) - %s) IS DISTINCT FROM (to_jsonb(NEW) - %s)) EXECUTE FUNCTION audit.record_change(%s)', table_name || '_after_update_audit', target, ignored, ignored, arguments ); END IF; EXECUTE format('DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS %I ON %s', table_name || '_after_truncate_audit', target); EXECUTE format( 'CREATE TRIGGER %I AFTER TRUNCATE ON %s FOR EACH STATEMENT EXECUTE FUNCTION audit.record_change()', table_name || '_after_truncate_audit', target ); END $function$
function: CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION util.guard_finalized_grade() RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'pg_temp' AS $function$ DECLARE request_id bigint; BEGIN IF OLD.grade_status <> 'finalized' THEN RETURN CASE WHEN TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN OLD ELSE NEW END; END IF; IF TG_OP = 'UPDATE' AND ( NEW.grade_status, NEW.grade_code, NEW.score, NEW.grade_points, NEW.counts_in_gpa, NEW.is_passed, NEW.grade_scale_id, NEW.status, NEW.credits, NEW.student_record_id, NEW.section_id, NEW.term_id, NEW.course_id, NEW.course_version_id ) IS NOT DISTINCT FROM ( OLD.grade_status, OLD.grade_code, OLD.score, OLD.grade_points, OLD.counts_in_gpa, OLD.is_passed, OLD.grade_scale_id, OLD.status, OLD.credits, OLD.student_record_id, OLD.section_id, OLD.term_id, OLD.course_id, OLD.course_version_id ) THEN RETURN NEW; END IF; request_id := NULLIF(current_setting('app.grade_change_request_id', true), '')::bigint; IF TG_OP = 'UPDATE' AND request_id IS NOT NULL AND EXISTS ( SELECT 1 FROM public.grade_change_requests AS r WHERE r.id = request_id AND r.enrollment_id = OLD.id AND r.status = 'approved' AND r.applied_at IS NULL ) AND NEW.grade_status = 'finalized' THEN RETURN NEW; END IF; RAISE EXCEPTION 'The finalized grade of enrollment % changes only through an approved grade change request.', OLD.id USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege'; END; $function$
function: CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION util.has_permission(permission_name text) RETURNS boolean LANGUAGE sql STABLE PARALLEL SAFE AS $function$ SELECT EXISTS ( SELECT 1 FROM public.role_assignments AS ra JOIN public.users AS u ON u.id = ra.user_id AND u.is_active JOIN public.role_has_permissions AS rhp ON rhp.role_id = ra.role_id JOIN public.permissions AS p ON p.id = rhp.permission_id WHERE ra.user_id = util.current_user_id() AND p.name = permission_name AND ra.scope_type = 'global' AND (ra.starts_at IS NULL OR ra.starts_at <= now()) AND (ra.ends_at IS NULL OR ra.ends_at > now()) ) $function$
function: CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION util.i18n(value jsonb) RETURNS text LANGUAGE sql STABLE PARALLEL SAFE AS $function$ SELECT util.i18n( value, COALESCE(NULLIF(current_setting('app.locale', true), ''), 'en'), COALESCE(NULLIF(current_setting('app.fallback_locale', true), ''), 'en') ) $function$
function: CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION util.i18n(value jsonb, locale text, fallback text DEFAULT 'en'::text) RETURNS text LANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE PARALLEL SAFE AS $function$ SELECT CASE WHEN value IS NULL OR jsonb_typeof(value) <> 'object' THEN NULL ELSE COALESCE( NULLIF(value ->> locale, ''), NULLIF(value ->> split_part(replace(locale, '-', '_'), '_', 1), ''), NULLIF(value ->> fallback, ''), (SELECT e.v FROM jsonb_each_text(value) AS e(k, v) WHERE e.v <> '' ORDER BY e.k LIMIT 1) ) END $function$
function: CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION util.i18n_search_text(value jsonb) RETURNS text LANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE PARALLEL SAFE AS $function$ SELECT COALESCE(string_agg(e.v, ' ' ORDER BY e.k), '') FROM jsonb_each_text(CASE WHEN jsonb_typeof(value) = 'object' THEN value ELSE '{}'::jsonb END) AS e(k, v) $function$
function: CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION util.instructor_meeting_conflict(instructor bigint, meeting_section bigint, meeting_weekday smallint, meeting_times int4range, meeting_dates daterange) RETURNS bigint LANGUAGE sql STABLE AS $function$ SELECT other.section_id FROM section_meetings AS other JOIN section_instructors AS si ON si.section_id = other.section_id WHERE si.instructor_id = instructor AND other.section_id <> meeting_section AND other.is_active AND other.weekday = meeting_weekday AND other.time_range && meeting_times AND other.date_range && meeting_dates LIMIT 1 $function$
function: CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION util.is_system_context() RETURNS boolean LANGUAGE sql STABLE PARALLEL SAFE AS $function$ SELECT COALESCE(util.current_actor() LIKE 'system:%', false) $function$
function: CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION util.org_unit_ancestor_ids(start_id bigint) RETURNS bigint[] LANGUAGE sql STABLE PARALLEL SAFE AS $function$ WITH RECURSIVE chain (id, parent_id, depth) AS ( SELECT o.id, o.parent_id, 0 FROM public.org_units AS o WHERE o.id = start_id UNION ALL SELECT parent.id, parent.parent_id, chain.depth + 1 FROM public.org_units AS parent JOIN chain ON parent.id = chain.parent_id WHERE chain.depth < 100 ) SELECT COALESCE(array_agg(chain.id ORDER BY chain.depth), '{}') FROM chain $function$
function: CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION util.org_unit_descendant_ids(root_ids bigint[]) RETURNS bigint[] LANGUAGE sql STABLE PARALLEL SAFE AS $function$ WITH RECURSIVE tree (id) AS ( SELECT o.id FROM public.org_units AS o WHERE o.id = ANY (root_ids) UNION SELECT child.id FROM public.org_units AS child JOIN tree ON child.parent_id = tree.id ) SELECT COALESCE(array_agg(tree.id), '{}') FROM tree $function$
function: CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION util.org_units_prevent_cycle() RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $function$ BEGIN IF NEW.parent_id IS NOT NULL AND NEW.parent_id = ANY (util.org_unit_descendant_ids(ARRAY[NEW.id])) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Org unit % cannot be placed under itself or one of its descendants', NEW.id USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation'; END IF; RETURN NEW; END $function$
function: CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION util.permission_org_unit_ids(permission_name text) RETURNS bigint[] LANGUAGE sql STABLE PARALLEL SAFE AS $function$ SELECT util.org_unit_descendant_ids(util.permission_scope_ids(permission_name, 'org_unit')) $function$
function: CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION util.permission_scope_ids(permission_name text, scope_type text) RETURNS bigint[] LANGUAGE sql STABLE PARALLEL SAFE AS $function$ SELECT COALESCE(array_agg(DISTINCT ra.scope_id), '{}') FROM public.role_assignments AS ra JOIN public.users AS u ON u.id = ra.user_id AND u.is_active JOIN public.role_has_permissions AS rhp ON rhp.role_id = ra.role_id JOIN public.permissions AS p ON p.id = rhp.permission_id WHERE ra.user_id = util.current_user_id() AND p.name = permission_name AND ra.scope_type = permission_scope_ids.scope_type AND (ra.starts_at IS NULL OR ra.starts_at <= now()) AND (ra.ends_at IS NULL OR ra.ends_at > now()) $function$
function: CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION util.section_instructors_check_meetings() RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $function$ DECLARE meeting record; conflicting bigint; BEGIN PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended('section_instructor:' || NEW.instructor_id, 0)); FOR meeting IN SELECT * FROM section_meetings WHERE section_id = NEW.section_id AND is_active LOOP conflicting := util.instructor_meeting_conflict(NEW.instructor_id, NEW.section_id, meeting.weekday, meeting.time_range, meeting.date_range); IF conflicting IS NOT NULL THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'instructor_conflict instructor=% section=% other_section=%', NEW.instructor_id, NEW.section_id, conflicting USING ERRCODE = 'exclusion_violation'; END IF; END LOOP; RETURN NEW; END $function$
function: CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION util.section_meetings_check_instructors() RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $function$ DECLARE instructor bigint; conflicting bigint; BEGIN IF NOT NEW.is_active THEN RETURN NEW; END IF; FOR instructor IN SELECT instructor_id FROM section_instructors WHERE section_id = NEW.section_id ORDER BY instructor_id LOOP PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended('section_instructor:' || instructor, 0)); conflicting := util.instructor_meeting_conflict(instructor, NEW.section_id, NEW.weekday, NEW.time_range, NEW.date_range); IF conflicting IS NOT NULL THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'instructor_conflict instructor=% section=% other_section=%', instructor, NEW.section_id, conflicting USING ERRCODE = 'exclusion_violation'; END IF; END LOOP; RETURN NEW; END $function$
function: CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION util.sections_sync_meeting_activity() RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $function$ BEGIN UPDATE section_meetings SET is_active = (NEW.status <> 'cancelled') WHERE section_id = NEW.id; RETURN NEW; END $function$
function: CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION util.student_number_exists(candidate text) RETURNS boolean LANGUAGE sql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'pg_temp' AS $function$ SELECT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.student_records WHERE student_number = candidate) $function$
function: CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION util.taught_student_record_ids() RETURNS SETOF bigint LANGUAGE sql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'pg_temp' AS $function$ SELECT e.student_record_id FROM public.enrollments AS e JOIN public.section_instructors AS si ON si.section_id = e.section_id JOIN public.instructors AS i ON i.id = si.instructor_id WHERE i.person_id = util.current_person_id() AND e.status IN ('enrolled', 'completed') $function$
function: CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION util.term_sessions_within_term() RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $function$ DECLARE term_start date; term_end date; BEGIN SELECT start_date, end_date INTO term_start, term_end FROM terms WHERE id = NEW.term_id; IF NEW.start_date < term_start OR NEW.end_date > term_end THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Term session % (% to %) must lie within its term (% to %)', NEW.code, NEW.start_date, NEW.end_date, term_start, term_end USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation'; END IF; RETURN NEW; END $function$
function: CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION util.user_id_for_institutional_email(candidate text) RETURNS bigint LANGUAGE sql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'pg_temp' AS $function$ SELECT u.id FROM public.student_records AS sr JOIN public.users AS u ON u.person_id = sr.person_id WHERE lower(sr.institutional_email) = lower(candidate) ORDER BY sr.admission_date DESC LIMIT 1 $function$
function: CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION util.user_id_for_student_number(candidate text, include_ended boolean) RETURNS bigint LANGUAGE sql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'pg_temp' AS $function$ SELECT u.id FROM public.student_records AS sr JOIN public.users AS u ON u.person_id = sr.person_id WHERE sr.student_number = candidate AND (include_ended OR sr.status IN ('enrolled', 'on_leave')) LIMIT 1 $function$

trigger: CREATE TRIGGER waitlist_entries_after_truncate_audit AFTER TRUNCATE ON public.waitlist_entries FOR EACH STATEMENT EXECUTE FUNCTION audit.record_change() enabled=O
trigger: CREATE TRIGGER waitlist_entries_after_write_audit AFTER INSERT OR DELETE OR UPDATE ON public.waitlist_entries FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION audit.record_change() enabled=O
view: reporting.course_grades SELECT e.id AS enrollment_id, e.student_record_id, sr.student_number, t.code AS term_code, util.i18n(t.name) AS term_name, c.code AS course_code, util.i18n(cv.title) AS course_title, cv.title AS course_title_i18n, s.section_code, e.credits, e.course_category_id, util.i18n(cc.name) AS course_category, e.status, e.grade_status, e.grade_code, e.is_passed, e.is_retake FROM enrollments e JOIN student_records sr ON sr.id = e.student_record_id JOIN terms t ON t.id = e.term_id JOIN courses c ON c.id = e.course_id JOIN course_versions cv ON cv.id = e.course_version_id JOIN sections s ON s.id = e.section_id LEFT JOIN course_categories cc ON cc.id = e.course_category_id;
view: reporting.degree_audits SELECT da.id AS degree_audit_id, da.student_record_id, sr.student_number, da.program_role, pr.code AS program_code, util.i18n(pr.name) AS program_name, da.is_satisfied, da.earned_credits, da.required_credits, da.cumulative_gpa, da.audited_at, t.code AS term_code FROM degree_audits da JOIN student_records sr ON sr.id = da.student_record_id JOIN programs pr ON pr.id = da.program_id LEFT JOIN terms t ON t.id = da.term_id WHERE (da.id IN ( SELECT max(degree_audits.id) AS max FROM degree_audits GROUP BY degree_audits.student_record_id, degree_audits.program_role));
view: reporting.headcount SELECT ou.id AS org_unit_id, ou.code AS org_unit_code, util.i18n(ou.name) AS org_unit_name, pr.id AS program_id, pr.code AS program_code, util.i18n(pr.name) AS program_name, sr.year_level, sr.status, count(*) AS students FROM student_records sr JOIN programs pr ON pr.id = sr.program_id JOIN org_units ou ON ou.id = sr.org_unit_id GROUP BY ou.id, ou.code, ou.name, pr.id, pr.code, pr.name, sr.year_level, sr.status;
view: reporting.section_enrollments SELECT s.id AS section_id, t.code AS term_code, util.i18n(t.name) AS term_name, c.code AS course_code, util.i18n(cv.title) AS course_title, s.section_code, ou.code AS org_unit_code, util.i18n(ou.name) AS org_unit_name, s.status, s.capacity, ( SELECT count(*) AS count FROM enrollments e WHERE e.section_id = s.id AND (e.status::text = ANY (ARRAY['enrolled'::character varying, 'completed'::character varying]::text[]))) AS enrolled, ( SELECT count(*) AS count FROM waitlist_entries w WHERE w.section_id = s.id AND w.status::text = 'waiting'::text) AS waiting, ( SELECT string_agg(COALESCE(util.i18n(ip.name_variants), ip.family_name::text || COALESCE(' '::text || ip.given_name::text, ''::text)), ', '::text ORDER BY si.role) AS string_agg FROM section_instructors si JOIN instructors i ON i.id = si.instructor_id JOIN people ip ON ip.id = i.person_id WHERE si.section_id = s.id) AS instructors FROM sections s JOIN terms t ON t.id = s.term_id JOIN courses c ON c.id = s.course_id JOIN course_versions cv ON cv.id = s.course_version_id JOIN org_units ou ON ou.id = s.org_unit_id;
view: reporting.section_meetings SELECT m.id AS section_meeting_id, s.id AS section_id, t.code AS term_code, c.code AS course_code, util.i18n(cv.title) AS course_title, s.section_code, m.weekday, m.start_time, m.end_time, m.start_date, m.end_date, b.code AS building_code, r.code AS room_code, m.is_active FROM section_meetings m JOIN sections s ON s.id = m.section_id JOIN terms t ON t.id = s.term_id JOIN courses c ON c.id = s.course_id JOIN course_versions cv ON cv.id = s.course_version_id LEFT JOIN rooms r ON r.id = m.room_id LEFT JOIN buildings b ON b.id = r.building_id;
view: reporting.status_changes SELECT sc.id AS status_change_id, sc.student_record_id, sr.student_number, sct.code AS type_code, util.i18n(sct.name) AS type_name, sc.from_status, sc.to_status, sc.effective_date, t.code AS term_code, sc.status, sc.requested_at, sc.decided_at FROM student_status_changes sc JOIN student_records sr ON sr.id = sc.student_record_id JOIN student_status_change_types sct ON sct.id = sc.status_change_type_id LEFT JOIN terms t ON t.id = sc.term_id;
view: reporting.students SELECT sr.id AS student_record_id, sr.student_number, sr.person_id, COALESCE(util.i18n(p.name_variants), p.family_name::text || COALESCE(' '::text || p.given_name::text, ''::text)) AS student_name, p.family_name, p.given_name, p.name_variants, p.gender, p.birth_date, sr.program_id, pr.code AS program_code, util.i18n(pr.name) AS program_name, pr.name AS program_name_i18n, sr.org_unit_id, ou.code AS org_unit_code, util.i18n(ou.name) AS org_unit_name, ou.name AS org_unit_name_i18n, sr.admission_date, sr.catalog_year, sr.year_level, sr.status, sr.academic_standing, sr.graduation_date, sr.degree_number, sr.institutional_email FROM student_records sr JOIN people p ON p.id = sr.person_id JOIN programs pr ON pr.id = sr.program_id JOIN org_units ou ON ou.id = sr.org_unit_id;
view: reporting.term_records SELECT tr.id AS term_record_id, tr.student_record_id, sr.student_number, t.id AS term_id, t.code AS term_code, util.i18n(t.name) AS term_name, tr.attempted_credits, tr.earned_credits, tr.term_gpa, tr.cumulative_earned_credits, tr.cumulative_gpa, tr.academic_standing, tr.warning_count, tr.is_honors, tr.honors_rank, tr.closed_at FROM term_records tr JOIN student_records sr ON sr.id = tr.student_record_id JOIN terms t ON t.id = tr.term_id;
